<!-- This QnA is a bit over the top, leaning heavily on QnA's ability to use JavaScipt. If you're editing this on the editor at QnAMarkup.org, its here so you can see how the settings control the look of the output and can turn on the saving of user progress. -->
Title: Zork QnA
Description: A port of Zork I to <a href="https://www.qnamarkup.org/">QnA Markup</a>, built from the game's <a href="https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/2025/11/20/preserving-code-that-shaped-generations-zork-i-ii-and-iii-go-open-source/" target="_blank">open source release</a>: all 110 rooms, the twenty treasures, 350 points, the troll, the thief and the cyclops. Like the Z-Machine before it, QnA let's us interpret different underlying files using one common specification. Nearly every line of room and response text is quoted from the Zork I source, released under an <a href="https://github.com/historicalsource/zork1/blob/master/LICENSE" target="_blank">MIT license</a>. The buttons are the commands you would have typed; they appear only when they make sense. Every button press is one move, and UNDO turns back the clock.
Before: <script>
/* Zork mini — runtime helpers, embedded by build.js into the QnA's Before: block.
 * Everything the markup calls lives on window.Z. State is kept in QnA variables, written as hidden
 * form fields inside the current bubble (Z.set) so UNDO, RESTART and saved progress rebuild it. */

  var LAMP_LIFE = [[100, 'The lamp appears a bit dimmer.'], [170, 'The lamp is definitely dimmer now.'], [185, 'The lamp is nearly out.'], [200, null]];
  var CANDLE_LIFE = [[20, 'The candles grow shorter.'], [30, 'The candles are becoming quite short.'], [35, "The candles won't last long now."], [40, null]];
  var DROWNINGS = ['up to your ankles.', 'up to your shin.', 'up to your knees.', 'up to your hips.', 'up to your waist.', 'up to your chest.', 'up to your neck.', 'over your head.', 'high in your lungs.'];

  /* --- variables ------------------------------------------------------ */
  function cur(n) { var v = I.getvar(n); return (v === undefined || v === null) ? '' : String(v); }
  function has(n) { return cur(n) !== '' && cur(n) !== '0'; }
  function num(n) { return parseInt(cur(n), 10) || 0; }
  function moves() { return I.history.length; }
  function frame() { var s = document.currentScript; return (s && s.closest) ? s.closest('.frame') : I.qanda.lastElementChild; }
  function set(o) {
    var f = frame();
    Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) {
      var v = o[k];
      if (typeof v === 'string' && /^[+-]=\d+$/.test(v)) v = String(num(k) + parseInt(v.replace('=', ''), 10));
      if (v === true) v = '1';
      if (v === false || v === null || v === undefined) v = '';
      var i = document.createElement('input'); i.type = 'hidden'; i.name = k; i.value = String(v); f.appendChild(i);
    });
    I.captureFields(I.qnum);
  }
  function say(h) { var s = document.currentScript, d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = h; s.parentNode.insertBefore(d, s); }
  function cond(e) {
    return e.split(',').every(function (t) {
      t = t.trim();
      var m = /^([a-z0-9_]+)(!?=)(.*)$/i.exec(t);
      if (m) { var want = m[3] === '@room' ? cur('room') : m[3]; return (cur(m[1]) === want) === (m[2] === '='); }
      return t.charAt(0) === '!' ? !has(t.slice(1)) : has(t);
    });
  }
  function go(target) { QnA.goto(target); }
  function back() { go(cur('room') || 'west_of_house'); }
  function roll(n) { return (moves() * 2654435761 % 1000) / 1000 < n; }   // a deterministic "random": same answer on replay

  /* --- items ---------------------------------------------------------- */
  function loc(id) { return cur('loc_' + id); }
  function carried() { return Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return loc(k) === 'me'; }); }
  function here(id) { return loc(id) === cur('room'); }
  function load() { return carried().reduce(function (s, k) { return s + (ITEMS[k].size || 5); }, 0); }
  function take(id, extra) {
    var it = ITEMS[id], o = extra || {};
    if (load() + (it.size || 5) > 100) { say('Your load is too heavy.'); return false; }
    if (id === 'rusty_knife' && loc('sword') === 'me') say('As you touch the rusty knife, your sword gives a single pulse of blinding blue light.');
    say('Taken.');
    o['loc_' + id] = 'me'; o['touched_' + id] = '1';
    if (it.tvalue && !has('got_' + id)) { o['got_' + id] = '1'; o.score = '+=' + it.tvalue; }
    set(o);
    return true;
  }
  function drop(id) { var o = {}; o['loc_' + id] = cur('room'); o['touched_' + id] = '1'; set(o); say('Dropped.'); }
  function deposit(id) {
    var it = ITEMS[id], o = {}; o['loc_' + id] = 'case'; o.score = '+=' + it.value; set(o); set({ case_desc: caseDesc() });
    if (num('score') >= 350 && !has('won')) { set({ won: '1', loc_map: 'case' }); say('An almost inaudible voice whispers in your ear, "Look to your treasures for the final secret."'); }
  }
  // what lies on the floor of this room (items away from where they started are also listed by the take menu)
  function floor() {
    var r = cur('room'), any = false;
    Object.keys(ITEMS).forEach(function (k) {
      var it = ITEMS[k];
      if (loc(k) !== r) return;
      if (it.hiddenIf && cond(it.hiddenIf)) return;
      if (r === 'treasure_room' && k !== 'chalice' && !has('thief_dead')) return;
      say(has('touched_' + k) || !it.fdesc ? it.ldesc : it.fdesc);
      if (k === 'bottle' && has('water')) say('The glass bottle contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;A quantity of water');
      if (k === 'coffin' && has('coffin_open') && loc('sceptre') === 'coffin') say('The gold coffin contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;A sceptre');
      if (k === 'egg' && has('egg_open') && loc('canary') === 'egg') say('The jewel-encrusted egg contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;A golden clockwork canary');
      if (k === 'buoy' && has('buoy_open') && loc('emerald') === 'buoy') say('The red buoy contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;A large emerald');
      if (it.home !== r) any = true;
    });
    set({ floor_any: any ? '1' : '' });
  }
  function takeMenu() {
    var r = cur('room'), list = Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return loc(k) === r && ITEMS[k].home !== r; });
    say(list.length ? 'You can see:<br>' + list.map(function (k) { return '&nbsp;&nbsp;' + ITEMS[k].name; }).join('<br>') : 'There is nothing here to take.');
  }
  function inventory() {
    var c = carried();
    if (!c.length) return 'You are empty-handed.';
    return 'You are carrying:<br>' + c.map(function (k) {
      var line = '&nbsp;&nbsp;' + ITEMS[k].name;
      if (k === 'lamp' && has('lamp_on')) line += ' (providing light)';
      if (k === 'torch') line += ' (providing light)';
      if (k === 'candles' && has('candles_lit')) line += ' (providing light)';
      if (k === 'bottle' && has('water')) line += '<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;The glass bottle contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;A quantity of water';
      if (k === 'egg' && has('egg_open') && loc('canary') === 'egg') line += '<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;The jewel-encrusted egg contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;A golden clockwork canary';
      if (k === 'buoy' && has('buoy_open') && loc('emerald') === 'buoy') line += '<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;The red buoy contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;A large emerald';
      if (k === 'coffin' && has('coffin_open') && loc('sceptre') === 'coffin') line += '<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;The gold coffin contains:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;A sceptre';
      return line;
    }).join('<br>');
  }
  function caseDesc() {
    var c = Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return loc(k) === 'case'; }).map(function (k) { return ITEMS[k].name; });
    return c.length ? 'Your collection of treasures consists of: ' + c.join(', ') + '.' : '';
  }
  function sackDesc() {
    var s = 'On the table is an elongated brown sack, smelling of hot peppers.';
    if (!has('sack_open')) return s;
    var c = []; if (loc('lunch') === 'sack') c.push('A lunch'); if (loc('garlic') === 'sack') c.push('A clove of garlic');
    return s + (c.length ? ' The brown sack contains: ' + c.join(', ') + '.' : ' The brown sack is empty.');
  }
  function mailboxDesc() { if (!has('mailbox_open')) return 'There is a small mailbox here.'; return loc('leaflet') === 'mailbox' ? 'The small mailbox contains: A leaflet.' : 'The small mailbox is open.'; }

  /* --- light, clock, tides -------------------------------------------- */
  function lightCarried() { return (loc('lamp') === 'me' && has('lamp_on')) || loc('torch') === 'me' || (loc('candles') === 'me' && has('candles_lit')); }
  function roomLit(id) {
    var r = ROOMS[id] || {};
    if (!r.dark) return id === 'maintenance_room' ? !has('maint_dark') : true;
    if (loc('torch') === id) return true;
    if (loc('candles') === id && has('candles_lit')) return true;
    if (id === 'grating_room' && has('grate_open')) return true;
    if (id === 'shaft_room' && has('basket_top') && basketLit()) return true;
    if (id === 'lower_shaft' && !has('basket_top') && basketLit()) return true;
    if (ROOMS[id].river && (id !== 'reservoir' || has('in_boat'))) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function lit(id) { return roomLit(id) || lightCarried(); }
  function tideLow() {
    var m = moves();
    if (has('gates_open')) return num('low_at') > 0 && m >= num('low_at');
    return num('high_at') > 0 && m < num('high_at');
  }
  function tide() {   // 'high' | 'falling' | 'low' | 'rising'
    if (has('gates_open')) return tideLow() ? 'low' : 'falling';
    return tideLow() ? 'rising' : 'high';
  }
  function tick(id) {
    var m = moves(), o = {}, msgs = [];
    if (cur('tick_at') === String(m)) return;   // one tick per move (the room is redrawn after inventory etc.)
    o.tick_at = String(m);
    if (loc('lamp') === 'me' && has('lamp_on')) {
      var u = num('lamp_used') + 1; o.lamp_used = String(u);
      LAMP_LIFE.forEach(function (p) { if (u === p[0]) { if (p[1]) msgs.push(p[1]); else { o.lamp_on = ''; o.lamp_dead = '1'; msgs.push("You'd better have more light than from the brass lantern."); } } });
    }
    if (has('candles_lit') && loc('candles') !== 'nowhere') {
      var c = num('candle_used') + 1; o.candle_used = String(c);
      CANDLE_LIFE.forEach(function (p) { if (c === p[0]) { if (p[1]) msgs.push(p[1]); else { o.candles_lit = ''; o.candles_dead = '1'; msgs.push("You'd better have more light than from the pair of candles."); } } });
    }
    if (has('leak_at') && !has('leak_fixed') && !has('maint_flooded') && m - num('leak_at') >= 14) o.maint_flooded = '1';
    if (num('wounds') > 0 && m - num('wound_at') >= 30) { o.wounds = String(num('wounds') - 1); o.wound_at = String(m); }
    set(o);
    set({ tide_low: tideLow() ? '1' : '', small_load: carried().every(function (k) { return (ITEMS[k].size || 5) <= 4; }) ? '1' : '' });
    msgs.forEach(say);
    if (has('thief_here') && cur('thief_room') !== id) set({ thief_here: '', thief_ko: '', thief_hp: '5' });
    thiefRoams(id);
    if (has('thief_here')) set({ thief_room: id });
    if (id === 'lower_shaft' && !has('shaft_lit') && lit(id)) set({ shaft_lit: '1', score: '+=13' });
  }
  function room(id, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var o = { room: id };
    if (opts.pts && !has('v_' + id)) { o['v_' + id] = '1'; o.score = '+=' + opts.pts; }
    if (has('in_boat')) o.loc_boat = id;
    set(o);
    tick(id);
    floor();
    if (!lit(id)) {
      // Zork shows nothing of a dark room; replace this bubble (and the rest of the description) with the warning
      var f = frame(), n = f.nextElementSibling;
      while (n && n.classList && n.classList.contains('frame') && n.getAttribute('data-exchange') === f.getAttribute('data-exchange')) { var nn = n.nextElementSibling; n.parentNode.removeChild(n); n = nn; }
      var qt = f.querySelector('.question_text'), s = document.currentScript;
      Array.prototype.slice.call(qt.childNodes).forEach(function (c) { if (c !== s && !(c.tagName === 'INPUT')) qt.removeChild(c); });
      say('It is pitch black. You are likely to be eaten by a grue.');
      Z.dark = true;
    } else Z.dark = false;
  }
  // a blocked direction: the usual message, or (in the dark) an 80% chance of the grue
  function blocked(text, roomId) {
    if (!lit(roomId) && roll(0.8)) { kill('Oh, no! You have walked into the slavering fangs of a lurking grue!'); return; }
    say(text); go(roomId);
  }
  function exit(target, c, text, roomId) { if (cond(c)) go(target); else { say(text); go(roomId); } }
  function kill(text) { say(text); go('died'); }

  /* --- puzzles -------------------------------------------------------- */
  function glow(level) { if (loc('sword') !== 'me' || has('troll_dead')) return; say(level === 2 ? 'Your sword is glowing very brightly.' : 'Your sword is glowing with a faint blue glow.'); }
  function fight() {
    var r = Math.random();
    if (r < 0.1) return go('t_die');
    if (has('troll_hp')) { if (r < 0.3) return go('t_miss'); if (r < 0.5) return go('t_graze'); return go('t_kill'); }
    if (r < 0.35) return go('t_miss'); if (r < 0.6) return go('t_parry'); if (r < 0.9) return go('t_wound'); return go('t_kill');
  }
  function chimney() {
    var n = carried().length;
    if (!n) { say('Going up empty-handed is a bad idea.'); return go('studio'); }
    if (loc('lamp') !== 'me' || n > 2) { say("You can't get up there with what you're carrying."); return go('studio'); }
    say('Up the dark and narrow chimney you go.');
    set({ trap_touched: '' }); go('kitchen');
  }
  function turnBolt() {
    if (!has('gate_flag')) { say("The bolt won't turn with your best effort."); return go('dam_room'); }
    if (has('gates_open')) { say('The sluice gates close and water starts to collect behind the dam.'); set({ gates_open: '', high_at: tideLow() ? String(moves() + 8) : '0', low_at: '0' }); }
    else { say('The sluice gates open and water pours through the dam.'); set({ gates_open: '1', low_at: String(moves() + 8), high_at: '0' }); }
    set({ tide_low: tideLow() ? '1' : '' });
    go('dam_room');
  }
  function damDesc() {
    var t = tide();
    say(t === 'low' ? 'The water level behind the dam is low: The sluice gates have been opened. Water rushes through the dam and downstream.' :
      t === 'falling' ? 'The sluice gates are open, and water rushes through the dam. The water level behind the dam is still high.' :
      t === 'rising' ? 'The sluice gates are closed. The water level in the reservoir is quite low, but the level is rising quickly.' :
      'The sluice gates on the dam are closed. Behind the dam, there can be seen a wide reservoir. Water is pouring over the top of the now abandoned dam.');
    say('There is a control panel here, on which a large metal bolt is mounted. Directly above the bolt is a small green plastic bubble' + (has('gate_flag') ? ' which is glowing serenely.' : '.'));
  }
  function reservoirSouthDesc() {
    var t = tide();
    say(t === 'low' ? 'You are in a long room, to the north of which was formerly a lake. However, with the water level lowered, there is merely a wide stream running through the center of the room.' :
      t === 'falling' ? 'You are in a long room. To the north is a large lake, too deep to cross. You notice, however, that the water level appears to be dropping at a rapid rate. Before long, it might be possible to cross to the other side from here.' :
      t === 'rising' ? 'You are in a long room, to the north of which is a wide area which was formerly a reservoir, but now is merely a stream. You notice, however, that the level of the stream is rising quickly and that before long it will be impossible to cross here.' :
      'You are in a long room on the south shore of a large lake, far too deep and wide for crossing.');
    say('There is a path along the stream to the east or west, a steep pathway climbing southwest along the edge of a chasm, and a path leading into a canyon to the southeast.');
  }
  function reservoirNorthDesc() {
    var t = tide();
    say(t === 'low' ? 'You are in a large cavernous room, the south of which was formerly a lake. However, with the water level lowered, there is merely a wide stream running through there.' :
      t === 'falling' ? 'You are in a large cavernous area. To the south is a wide lake, whose water level appears to be falling rapidly.' :
      t === 'rising' ? 'You are in a cavernous area, to the south of which is a very wide stream. The level of the stream is rising rapidly, and it appears that before long it will be impossible to cross to the other side.' :
      'You are in a large cavernous room, north of a large lake.');
    say('There is a slimy stairway leaving the room to the north.');
  }
  function reservoirDesc() {
    if (has('in_boat')) {
      if (tideLow()) say('The water level has dropped to the point at which the boat can no longer stay afloat. It sinks into the mud.');
      else { say('You are on the lake. Beaches can be seen north and south. Upstream a small stream enters the lake through a narrow cleft in the rocks. The dam can be seen downstream.'); return; }
    }
    // on foot you only stand here at low tide; if the water comes back while you are here, Zork drowns you
    if (!tideLow()) { kill('You are lifted up by the rising river! You try to swim, but the currents are too strong. You come closer, closer to the awesome structure of Flood Control Dam #3. The dam beckons to you. The roar of the water nearly deafens you, but you remain conscious as you tumble over the dam toward your certain doom among the rocks at its base.'); return; }
    say('You are on what used to be a large lake, but which is now a large mud pile. There are "shores" to the north and south.');
    if (tide() === 'rising') say('You notice that the water level here is rising rapidly. The currents are also becoming stronger. Staying here seems quite perilous!');
  }
  function deepCanyonDesc() {
    var t = tide();
    say('You are on the south edge of a deep canyon. Passages lead off to the east, northwest and southwest. A stairway leads down.' + (t === 'falling' ? ' You can hear a loud roaring sound, like that of rushing water, from below.' : t === 'rising' ? '' : ' You can hear the sound of flowing water from below.'));
  }
  function loudRoom() {
    var t = tide();
    if (t === 'falling') {
      say('It is unbearably loud here, with an ear-splitting roar seeming to come from all around you. There is a pounding in your head which won\'t stop. With a tremendous effort, you scramble out of the room.');
      var runs = ['damp_cave', 'round_room', 'deep_canyon'];
      return go(runs[moves() % 3]);
    }
    var quiet = has('loud_quiet') || t === 'rising';
    set({ loud_ok: quiet ? '1' : '' });
    say(quiet ? 'The room is eerie in its quietness.' : 'The room is deafeningly loud with an undetermined rushing sound. The sound seems to reverberate from all of the walls, making it difficult even to think.');
  }
  function maintenance() {
    if (has('maint_flooded')) return;
    if (has('leak_at') && !has('leak_fixed')) {
      var lvl = moves() - num('leak_at');
      if (lvl >= 14) { kill('I\'m afraid you have done drowned yourself.'); return; }
      say('The water level here is now ' + DROWNINGS[Math.min(Math.floor(lvl / 2), DROWNINGS.length - 1)]);
    }
  }
  function pressButton(colour) {
    if (colour === 'blue') { if (has('leak_at')) say('The blue button appears to be jammed.'); else { say('There is a rumbling sound and a stream of water appears to burst from the east wall of the room (apparently, a leak has occurred in a pipe).'); set({ leak_at: String(moves()) }); } }
    if (colour === 'red') { say('The lights within the room ' + (has('maint_dark') ? 'come on.' : 'shut off.')); set({ maint_dark: has('maint_dark') ? '' : '1' }); }
    if (colour === 'yellow') { say('Click.'); set({ gate_flag: '1' }); }
    if (colour === 'brown') { say('Click.'); set({ gate_flag: '' }); }
    go('maintenance_room');
  }
  function hades() {
    var m = moves();
    if (has('bell_cool_at') && m >= num('bell_cool_at')) { set({ bell_cool_at: '', bell_hot: '', loc_bell: 'entrance_to_hades' }); say('The bell appears to have cooled down.'); }
    if (!has('lld_flag')) {
      if (has('xb_at') && !has('xc_at') && m > num('xb_at') + 6) { set({ xb_at: '' }); say('The tension of this ceremony is broken, and the wraiths, amused but shaken at your clumsy attempt, resume their hideous jeering.'); }
      if (has('xc_at') && m > num('xc_at') + 3) { set({ xc_at: '', xb_at: '' }); say('The tension of this ceremony is broken, and the wraiths, amused but shaken at your clumsy attempt, resume their hideous jeering.'); }
      if (has('xb_at') && !has('xc_at') && loc('candles') === 'me' && has('candles_lit')) { set({ xc_at: String(m) }); say('The flames flicker wildly and appear to dance. The earth beneath your feet trembles, and your legs nearly buckle beneath you. The spirits cower at your unearthly power.'); }
    }
    say(has('lld_flag') ? 'The gate is open; through it you can see a desolation, with a pile of mangled bodies in one corner. Thousands of voices, lamenting some hideous fate, can be heard.' : 'The gate is open; through it you can see a desolation, with a pile of mangled bodies in one corner. Thousands of voices, lamenting some hideous fate, can be heard.<br>The way through the gate is barred by evil spirits, who jeer at your attempts to pass.');
    if (has('bell_hot')) say('On the ground is a red hot bell.');
  }
  function ringBell() {
    if (has('lld_flag')) { say('Ding, dong.'); return go('entrance_to_hades'); }
    say('The bell suddenly becomes red hot and falls to the ground. The wraiths, as if paralyzed, stop their jeering and slowly turn to face you. On their ashen faces, the expression of a long-forgotten terror takes shape.');
    var o = { loc_bell: 'hot', bell_hot: '1', bell_cool_at: String(moves() + 20), xb_at: String(moves()), xc_at: '' };
    if (loc('candles') === 'me') { say('In your confusion, the candles drop to the ground (and they are out).'); o.loc_candles = 'entrance_to_hades'; o.touched_candles = '1'; o.candles_lit = ''; }
    set(o); go('entrance_to_hades');
  }
  function readBook() {
    if (cur('room') === 'entrance_to_hades' && has('xc_at') && !has('lld_flag')) {
      say('Each word of the prayer reverberates through the hall in a deafening confusion. As the last word fades, a voice, loud and commanding, speaks: "Begone, fiends!" A heart-stopping scream fills the cavern, and the spirits, sensing a greater power, flee through the walls.');
      set({ lld_flag: '1', xb_at: '', xc_at: '' });
    } else {
      say('Commandment #12592<br><br>Oh ye who go about saying unto each: "Hello sailor":<br>Dost thou know the magnitude of thy sin before the gods?<br>Yea, verily, thou shalt be ground between two stones.<br>Shall the angry gods cast thy body into the whirlpool?<br>Surely, thy eye shall be put out with a sharp stick!<br>Even unto the ends of the earth shalt thou wander and<br>Unto the land of the dead shalt thou be sent at last.<br>Surely thou shalt repent of thy cunning.');
    }
    back();
  }
  function lightCandles(withWhat) {
    if (withWhat === 'torch') { if (has('candles_lit')) say('You realize, just in time, that the candles are already lighted.'); else { say('The heat from the torch is so intense that the candles are vaporized.'); set({ loc_candles: 'nowhere' }); } return back(); }
    if (num('matches') <= 0) { say("I'm afraid that you have run out of matches."); return back(); }
    set({ matches: '-=1' });
    if (cur('room') === 'gas_room') { say('How sad for an aspiring adventurer to light a match in a room which reeks of gas. Fortunately, there is justice in the world.'); return kill('<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;** BOOOOOOOOOOOM **'); }
    say('One of the matches starts to burn.');
    if (has('candles_dead')) say("Alas, there's not much left of the candles. Certainly not enough to burn.");
    else { say('The candles are lit.'); set({ candles_lit: '1' }); }
    back();
  }
  function tinyCave() { if (loc('candles') === 'me' && has('candles_lit') && roll(0.5)) { set({ candles_lit: '' }); say('A gust of wind blows out your candles!'); if (!lit('tiny_cave')) say('It is now completely dark.'); } }
  function mirror(here) {
    var other = here === 'mirror_room_1' ? 'mirror_room_2' : 'mirror_room_1';
    if (has('mirror_broken')) { say('Fiddling with the mirror has no effect.'); return go(here); }
    say('There is a rumble from deep within the earth and the room shakes.'); go(other);
  }
  function altarDown() {
    if (loc('coffin') === 'me') { say("You haven't a prayer of getting the coffin down there."); return go('south_temple'); }
    go('tiny_cave');
  }
  function rank(s) { return s === 350 ? 'Master Adventurer' : s > 330 ? 'Wizard' : s > 300 ? 'Master' : s > 200 ? 'Adventurer' : s > 100 ? 'Junior Adventurer' : s > 50 ? 'Novice Adventurer' : s > 25 ? 'Amateur Adventurer' : 'Beginner'; }
  function score() { var s = num('score'), m = moves(); return 'Your score is ' + s + ' (total of 350 points), in ' + m + (m === 1 ? ' move.' : ' moves.') + '<br>This gives you the rank of ' + rank(s) + '.'; }
  function die() {
    if (has('mirror_broken')) say('Bad luck, huh?');
    set({ deaths: '+=1', score: '-=10' });
    if (num('deaths') >= 3) return go('maniac');
    var o = { lamp_on: '', candles_lit: '', in_boat: '', wounds: '0', thief_here: '' };
    carried().forEach(function (k) { o['loc_' + k] = ITEMS[k].home; });
    set(o);
    set({ sack_desc: sackDesc() });
    go('resurrect');
  }

  /* --- the rest of the map: thief, cyclops, mine, river, endgame ----- */
  var RIVER = {"NEXT":{"river_1":"river_2","river_2":"river_3","river_3":"river_4","river_4":"river_5","river_5":"falls"},"SPEED":{"river_1":4,"river_2":4,"river_3":3,"river_4":2,"river_5":1}};
  var HERO_MISS = ['Your sword misses the thief by an inch.', 'A good slash, but it misses the thief by a mile.', 'You charge, but the thief jumps nimbly aside.', 'Clang! Crash! The thief parries.'];
  var THIEF_MISS = ['The thief stabs nonchalantly with his stiletto and misses.', 'You dodge as the thief comes in low.', 'You parry a lightning thrust, and the thief salutes you with a grim nod.', 'The thief tries to sneak past your guard, but you twist away.'];
  var THIEF_WOUND = ['A quick thrust pinks your left arm, and blood starts to trickle down.', 'The thief draws blood, raking his stiletto across your arm.', 'The stiletto flashes faster than you can follow, and blood wells from your leg.', 'The thief strikes like a snake! The resulting wound is serious.'];
  var THIEF_KILL = ['Finishing you off, the thief inserts his blade into your heart.', 'The thief comes in from the side, feints, and inserts the blade into your ribs.', 'The thief, forgetting his essentially genteel upbringing, cuts your throat.'];
  var CYCLOMAD = ['The cyclops seems somewhat agitated.', 'The cyclops appears to be getting more agitated.', 'The cyclops is moving about the room, looking for something.', 'The cyclops was looking for salt and pepper. No doubt they are condiments for his upcoming snack.', 'The cyclops is moving toward you in an unfriendly manner.', 'You have two choices: 1. Leave  2. Become dinner.'];
  var BAT_DROPS = ['mine_1', 'mine_2', 'mine_3', 'mine_4', 'ladder_top', 'ladder_bottom', 'squeaky_room', 'mine_entrance'];
  var BDIGS = ['You seem to be digging a hole here.', 'The hole is getting deeper, but that\'s about it.', 'You are surrounded by a wall of sand on all sides.'];
  var SACRED = { coffin: 1, bar: 1 };   // the thief leaves these alone (SACREDBIT)
  function pick(list, n) { return list[Math.abs(n) % list.length]; }
  function treasuresOn(where) { return Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return ITEMS[k].tvalue && !SACRED[k] && loc(k) === where; }); }
  function rob() {
    var took = treasuresOn('me').concat(treasuresOn(cur('room'))), o = {};
    took.forEach(function (k) { o['loc_' + k] = 'thief'; o['touched_' + k] = '1'; });
    if (took.length) set(o);
    return took.length > 0;
  }
  function thiefRoams(id) {
    if (has('thief_dead') || !ROOMS[id].dark || id === 'treasure_room' || id === 'cyclops_room' || id === 'troll_room' && !has('troll_dead')) return;
    if (!lit(id)) return;
    var m = moves();
    if (has('thief_here')) {
      if (has('thief_ko')) return;
      if (roll(0.3)) {
        var r = rob();
        say(r ? 'The thief just left, still carrying his large bag. You may not have noticed that he robbed you blind first.' : 'The thief, finding nothing of value, left disgusted.');
        set({ thief_here: '', thief_hp: '5' });
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!roll(0.12)) return;
    if ((m * 7) % 10 < 4) {
      say('Someone carrying a large bag is casually leaning against one of the walls here. He does not speak, but it is clear from his aspect that the bag will be taken only over his dead body.');
      set({ thief_here: '1', thief_ko: '' });
    } else if (rob()) {
      say('A seedy-looking individual with a large bag just wandered through the room. On the way through, he quietly abstracted some valuables from your possession, mumbling something about "Doing unto others before..."');
    } else {
      say('A "lean and hungry" gentleman just wandered through, carrying a large bag. Finding nothing of value, he left disgruntled.');
    }
  }
  function lair() {
    if (has('thief_dead')) return;
    if (!has('thief_here')) say('You hear a scream of anguish as you violate the robber\'s hideaway. Using passages unknown to you, he rushes to its defense.');
    var hidden = Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'chalice' && loc(k) === 'treasure_room'; });
    if (hidden.length) say('The thief gestures mysteriously, and the treasures in the room suddenly vanish.');
    say('There is a suspicious-looking individual, holding a bag, leaning against one wall. He is armed with a vicious-looking stiletto.');
    set({ thief_here: '1', thief_ko: '', thief_room: 'treasure_room' });
  }
  function thiefDies() {
    var o = { thief_dead: '1', thief_here: '', thief_ko: '', loc_stiletto: cur('room'), touched_stiletto: '1' }, r = cur('room'), loot = [];
    Object.keys(ITEMS).forEach(function (k) { if (loc(k) === 'thief') { o['loc_' + k] = r; o['touched_' + k] = '1'; loot.push(k); if (k === 'egg') o.egg_open = '1'; } });
    set(o);
    if (r === 'treasure_room') {
      var shown = Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'chalice' && loc(k) === r; });
      if (shown.length) say('As the thief dies, the power of his magic decreases, and his treasures reappear:<br>' + shown.map(function (k) { return '&nbsp;&nbsp;' + ITEMS[k].name + (k === 'egg' && loc('canary') === 'egg' ? ', with a golden clockwork canary' : ''); }).join('<br>'));
      say('The chalice is now safe to take.');
    } else if (loot.length) say('His booty remains.');
  }
  // An answer script has no bubble to write into, so the roll is taken here and the round is played
  // out by Q(thief_round); the rolls are kept in variables so UNDO and restore replay the same round.
  function fightThief(weapon) { Z.pending = { w: weapon, r: Math.random(), r2: Math.random() }; go('thief_round'); }
  function thiefRound() {
    var p = Z.pending; Z.pending = null;
    if (p) set({ fight_w: p.w, fight_r: String(p.r), fight_r2: String(p.r2) });
    var weapon = cur('fight_w') || 'sword', r = parseFloat(cur('fight_r')) || 0, r2 = parseFloat(cur('fight_r2')) || 0, m = moves();
    var hp = num('thief_hp'), here = cur('room');
    if (has('thief_ko')) { say('The unconscious thief cannot defend himself: He dies.'); thiefDies(); return go(here); }
    if (r < 0.3) say(pick(HERO_MISS, m).replace('sword', weapon === 'knife' ? 'nasty knife' : 'sword'));
    else if (r < 0.6) { hp -= 1; say(pick(['The thief is struck on the arm; blood begins to trickle down.', 'Your ' + weapon + ' pinks the thief on the wrist, but it\'s not serious.', 'The blow lands, making a shallow gash in the thief\'s arm!'], m)); }
    else if (r < 0.8) { hp -= 2; say(pick(['The thief receives a deep gash in his side.', 'A savage blow on the thigh! The thief is stunned but can still fight!', 'Slash! Your stroke connects! The thief could be down with one more blow!'], m)); }
    else if (r < 0.9) { say('Your ' + weapon + ' crashes down, knocking the thief into dreamland.'); set({ thief_ko: '1', thief_hp: '0' }); return go(here); }
    else { hp = 0; say('It\'s curtains for the thief as your ' + weapon + ' removes his head.'); }
    if (hp <= 0) { set({ thief_hp: '0' }); thiefDies(); return go(here); }
    set({ thief_hp: String(hp) });
    if (hp <= 2 && here !== 'treasure_room' && r2 < 0.5) {
      say('Your opponent, determining discretion to be the better part of valor, decides to terminate this little contretemps. With a rueful nod of his head, he steps backward into the gloom and disappears.');
      set({ thief_here: '', thief_hp: '5' });
      return go(here);
    }
    if (r2 < 0.5) say(pick(THIEF_MISS, m + 1));
    else if (r2 < 0.85) {
      var w = num('wounds') + 1;
      if (w >= 3) { say(pick(THIEF_KILL, m)); return go('died'); }
      say(pick(THIEF_WOUND, m)); set({ wounds: String(w), wound_at: String(m) });
    } else say(pick(['The butt of his stiletto cracks you on the skull, and you stagger back.', 'The thief, a man of superior breeding, pauses for a moment to consider the propriety of finishing you off.', 'The thief amuses himself by searching your pockets.'], m));
    go(here);
  }
  function giveThief(id) {
    var o = {}; o['loc_' + id] = 'thief'; o['touched_' + id] = '1'; set(o);
    if (ITEMS[id].tvalue) say('The thief is taken aback by your unexpected generosity, but accepts the ' + ITEMS[id].name.replace(/^A(n)? /, '') + ' and stops to admire its beauty.');
    else say('The thief places the ' + ITEMS[id].name.replace(/^A(n)? /, '') + ' in his bag and thanks you politely.');
    back();
  }
  function diagnose() {
    var w = num('wounds');
    var s = w === 0 ? 'You are in perfect health.' : 'You have ' + (w === 1 ? 'a light wound,' : w === 2 ? 'a serious wound,' : 'several wounds,') + ' which will be cured after ' + (30 - (moves() - num('wound_at'))) + ' moves.';
    s += ' You can ' + (w >= 2 ? 'be killed by one more light wound.' : w === 1 ? 'survive one serious wound.' : 'survive several wounds.');
    if (num('deaths') > 0) s += ' You have been killed ' + (num('deaths') === 1 ? 'once.' : 'twice.');
    say(s); back();
  }
  function curse() {
    var o = {}; treasuresOn('me').concat(treasuresOn(cur('room'))).forEach(function (k) { o['loc_' + k] = 'land_of_living_dead'; o['touched_' + k] = '1'; });
    if (Object.keys(o).length) set(o);
  }
  function cyclops() {
    if (has('magic')) { say('The east wall, previously solid, now has a cyclops-sized opening in it.'); return; }
    if (has('cyclops_asleep')) { say('The cyclops is sleeping blissfully at the foot of the stairs.'); return; }
    var w = num('cyc_wrath');
    if (w === 0) say('A cyclops, who looks prepared to eat horses (much less mere adventurers), blocks the staircase. From his state of health, and the bloodstains on the walls, you gather that he is not very friendly, though he likes people.');
    else if (w > 0) say('The cyclops is standing in the corner, eyeing you closely. I don\'t think he likes you very much. He looks extremely hungry, even for a cyclops.');
    else say('The cyclops, having eaten the hot peppers, appears to be gasping. His enflamed tongue protrudes from his man-sized mouth.');
    if (cur('cyc_tick') === String(moves())) return;
    set({ cyc_tick: String(moves()) });
    w = w < 0 ? w - 1 : w + 1;
    if (Math.abs(w) > 5) { kill('The cyclops, tired of all of your games and trickery, grabs you firmly. As he licks his chops, he says "Mmm. Just like Mom used to make \'em." It\'s nice to be appreciated.'); return; }
    set({ cyc_wrath: String(w) });
    say(CYCLOMAD[Math.abs(w) - 1]);
  }
  function feedCyclops(what) {
    var w = num('cyc_wrath');
    if (what === 'lunch') {
      if (w >= 0) { say('The cyclops says "Mmm Mmm. I love hot peppers! But oh, could I use a drink. Perhaps I could drink the blood of that thing."  From the gleam in his eye, it could be surmised that you are "that thing".'); set({ loc_lunch: 'nowhere', cyc_wrath: String(Math.min(-1, -w)) }); }
      else say('The cyclops is not so stupid as to eat THAT!');
    } else {
      if (w < 0) { say('The cyclops takes the bottle, checks that it\'s open, and drinks the water. A moment later, he lets out a yawn that nearly blows you over, and then falls fast asleep (what did you put in that drink, anyway?).'); set({ water: '', bottle_open: '1', loc_bottle: 'cyclops_room', cyclops_asleep: '1' }); }
      else say('The cyclops apparently is not thirsty and refuses your generous offer.');
    }
    go('cyclops_room');
  }
  function bats() {
    if (loc('garlic') === 'me' || loc('garlic') === 'bat_room') return;

    go(pick(BAT_DROPS, moves() * 3));
  }
  function flameCarried() { return loc('torch') === 'me' || (loc('candles') === 'me' && has('candles_lit')); }
  function gasRoom() {
    if (!flameCarried()) return;

    kill('<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;** BOOOOOOOOOOOM **');
  }
  function inBasket() { return Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return loc(k) === 'basket'; }); }
  function basketHere() {
    var r = cur('room'), top = has('basket_top');
    if ((r === 'shaft_room' && top) || (r === 'lower_shaft' && !top)) {
      say(top ? 'At the end of the chain is a basket.' : 'From the chain is suspended a basket.');
      var c = inBasket(); if (c.length) say('The basket contains:<br>' + c.map(function (k) { return '&nbsp;&nbsp;' + ITEMS[k].name; }).join('<br>'));
    }
  }
  function basketLit() { return inBasket().some(function (k) { return k === 'torch' || (k === 'lamp' && has('lamp_on')) || (k === 'candles' && has('candles_lit')); }); }
  function basketMenu(mode) {
    var list = mode === 'put' ? carried() : inBasket();

  }
  function basketPut(id) { var o = {}; o['loc_' + id] = 'basket'; set(o); say('Done.'); back(); }
  function machineDesc() { }
  function openLid() {
    var c = Object.keys(ITEMS).filter(function (k) { return loc(k) === 'machine'; });
    say(c.length ? 'The lid opens, revealing ' + c.map(function (k) { return ITEMS[k].name.replace(/^A(n)? /, 'a '); }).join(', ') + '.' : 'The lid opens.');

  }
  function runMachine() {
    if (has('machine_open')) { say('The machine doesn\'t seem to want to do anything.'); return go('machine_room'); }
    say('The machine comes to life (figuratively) with a dazzling display of colored lights and bizarre noises. After a few moments, the excitement abates.');
    var o = {};
    if (loc('coal') === 'machine') { o.loc_coal = 'nowhere'; o.loc_diamond = 'machine'; }
    else Object.keys(ITEMS).forEach(function (k) { if (loc(k) === 'machine') o['loc_' + k] = 'nowhere'; });
    set(o); go('machine_room');
  }
  function board() {
    var sharp = ['sword', 'knife', 'sceptre', 'rusty_knife', 'stiletto'].some(function (k) { return loc(k) === 'me'; });
    if (sharp) { say('Oops! Something sharp seems to have slipped and punctured the boat. The boat deflates to the sounds of hissing, sputtering, and cursing.'); set({ loc_boat: 'nowhere', loc_pboat: cur('room'), touched_pboat: '1' }); return back(); }
    say('You are now in the magic boat.'); set({ in_boat: '1' }); back();
  }
  function river() {
    var r = cur('room');
    if (!has('in_boat')) { kill('You splash around for a while, fighting the current, then you drown.'); return; }
    if (cur('drift_room') !== r) { set({ drift_room: r, drift_at: String(moves() + RIVER.SPEED[r]) }); return; }
    if (moves() >= num('drift_at')) {
      var next = RIVER.NEXT[cur('room')];
      if (next === 'falls') { kill('Unfortunately, the magic boat doesn\'t provide protection from the rocks and boulders one meets at the bottom of waterfalls. Including this one.'); return; }
      say('The flow of the river carries you downstream.');
      set({ drift_room: next, drift_at: String(moves() + RIVER.SPEED[next]) });
      go(next);
    }
  }
  function buoyCheck() { if (loc('buoy') === 'me' && !has('buoy_noticed')) { set({ buoy_noticed: '1' }); say('You notice something funny about the feel of the buoy.'); } }
  function dig() {
    var d = num('digs') + 1; set({ digs: String(d) });
    if (d > 3) { kill('The hole collapses, smothering you.'); return; }
    if (d === 3) { say('You can see a scarab here in the sand.'); set({ scarab_found: '1' }); }
    else say(BDIGS[d - 1]);
    go(cur('room'));
  }
  function waveSceptre() {
    var r = cur('room');
    if (!has('rainbow')) {
      say('Suddenly, the rainbow appears to become solid and, I venture, walkable (I think the giveaway was the stairs and bannister).');
      if (r === 'end_of_rainbow' && loc('pot') === 'end_of_rainbow') say('A shimmering pot of gold appears at the end of the rainbow.');
      set({ rainbow: '1' });
    } else { say('The rainbow seems to have become somewhat run-of-the-mill.'); set({ rainbow: '' }); }
    go(r);
  }
  function windCanary() {
    var r = cur('room'), forest = ['forest_1', 'forest_2', 'forest_3', 'forest_path', 'clearing_n', 'clearing_e', 'up_a_tree', 'mountains'].indexOf(r) >= 0;
    if (forest && !has('sing_song')) {
      say('The canary chirps, slightly off-key, an aria from a forgotten opera. From out of the greenery flies a lovely songbird. It perches on a limb just over your head and opens its beak to sing. As it does so a beautiful brass bauble drops from its mouth, bounces off the top of your head, and lands glimmering in the grass. As the canary winds down, the songbird flies away.');
      set({ sing_song: '1', loc_bauble: r === 'up_a_tree' ? 'forest_path' : r, touched_bauble: '1' });
    } else say('The canary chirps blithely, if somewhat tinnily, for a short time.');
    back();
  }

  var rc = I.renderChoices;
  I.renderChoices = function () {
    rc.apply(this, arguments);
    var bs = this.choices.querySelectorAll('.qabutton'), q = this.current !== null ? this.byLabel[this.current] : null, inRoom = !!(q && ROOMS[q.name]);

      if (hide) bs[i].style.display = 'none';
    }
  };

Q(inside_barrow):Inside the Barrow<br>As you enter the barrow, the door closes inexorably behind you. Around you it is dark, but ahead is an enormous cavern, brightly lit. Through its center runs a wide stream. Spanning the stream is a small wooden footbridge, and beyond a path leads into a dark tunnel. Above the bridge, floating in the air, is a large sign. It reads:  All ye who stand before this bridge have completed a great and perilous adventure which has tested your wit and courage. You have mastered the first part of the ZORK trilogy. Those who pass over this bridge must be prepared to undertake an even greater adventure that will severely test your skill and bravery!<br><br>The ZORK trilogy continues with "ZORK II: The Wizard of Frobozz" and is completed in "ZORK III: The Dungeon Master."<br><br><script>Z.say(Z.score())</script> GOTO:thanks

Q(read_leaflet):"WELCOME TO ZORK!<br><br>ZORK is a game of adventure, danger, and low cunning. In it you will explore some of the most amazing territory ever seen by mortals. No computer should be without one!"<script>Z.back()</script>

Q(read_guidebook):"&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Flood Control Dam #3<br><br>FCD#3 was constructed in year 783 of the Great Underground Empire to harness the mighty Frigid River. This work was supported by a grant of 37 million zorkmids from your omnipotent local tyrant Lord Dimwit Flathead the Excessive. This impressive structure is composed of 370,000 cubic feet of concrete, is 256 feet tall at the center, and 193 feet wide at the top. The lake created behind the dam has a volume of 1.7 billion cubic feet, an area of 12 million square feet, and a shore line of 36 thousand feet.<br><br>The construction of FCD#3 took 112 days from ground breaking to the dedication. It required a work force of 384 slaves, 34 slave drivers, 12 engineers, 2 turtle doves, and a partridge in a pear tree. The work was managed by a command team composed of 2345 bureaucrats, 2347 secretaries (at least two of whom could type), 12,256 paper shufflers, 52,469 rubber stampers, 245,193 red tape processors, and nearly one million dead trees.<br><br>We will now point out some of the more interesting features of FCD#3 as we conduct you on a guided tour of the facilities:<br><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;1) You start your tour here in the Dam Lobby. You will notice on your right that...."<script>Z.back()</script>

Q(read_matchbook):(Close cover before striking)<br><br>YOU too can make BIG MONEY in the exciting field of PAPER SHUFFLING!<br><br>Mr. Anderson of Muddle, Mass. says: "Before I took this course I was a lowly bit twiddler. Now with what I learned at GUE Tech I feel really important and can obfuscate and confuse with the best."<br><br>Dr. Blank had this to say: "Ten short days ago all I could look forward to was a dead-end job as a doctor. Now I have a promising future and make really big Zorkmids."<br><br>GUE Tech can't promise these fantastic results to everyone. But when you earn your degree from GUE Tech, your future will be brighter.<script>Z.back()</script>
